Title: Prophetical Warnings of Elias Marion, heretofore one of the Commanders of the Protestants that had taken arms in the Cevennes; or, Discourses uttered by him in London, under the operation of the Spirit
Creator: Élie Marion; English translator not named on the title page
Publisher: London (the imprint is not legible in the scan)
Publication Date: 1707
Rights: Public Domain (published 1707, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item bim_eighteenth-century_prophetical-warnings-of-_marion-lie_1707 (https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_prophetical-warnings-of-_marion-lie_1707), the item's own bim_eighteenth-century_prophetical-warnings-of-_marion-lie_1707_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Internet Archive; host metadata date: 1707. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: d2c6c207e5a6769c7119af42b0ac9f0de45ed7733f9e42db22044aaa9bef88b1.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. British Library microfilm scan (Eighteenth Century Collections Online); the opening leaves are garbled and some pages within are badly read; most warnings are readable with the long s kept.
Content note: The contemporary English edition of Marion's warnings, published in London in the same year as the French.
